Row schema (`heldout_confirm` / `heldout_samples`)\n\n- **`input`**: a JSON string `{\"sentence\", \"candidate_fol\"}`, where `candidate_fol` is the extracted formula line.\n- **`output`**: `faithful` | `unfaithful` | `unknown`.\n\nFinal-label precedence:\n1. **L3 panel majority** (`label_source=panel3`, 609 greedy rows).\n2. For ProverQA with gold the panel judged faithful: **L1 vs the original gold** (`equiv_synthetic_gold`).\n3. Otherwise: **L1 vs audited gold** (`equiv_audited_gold`). `equiv_proved`/`equiv_bounded` map to faithful; `non_equiv`/`non_equiv_no_bijection` map to unfaithful.\n4. Otherwise `unknown` (453 greedy rows): unparseable candidates, `no_audited_gold` (the panel's correction failed to parse or verify) and `unknown_capped`. Unknown rows are kept and counted.\n\nMain metadata fields:\n- **Provenance**: `corpus`, `corpus_subset`, `source_id`, `story_id`, `licence`, `system`, `model`, `system_tier` (required/extra), `provider`, `sample_idx`, `temperature`, `raw_output`, `parse_ok`, `parse_error`, `parse_notes`, `finish_reason`, `prompt_sha1`, `extractor_version`, `gen_usd`, `gen_seconds`.\n- **Gold**:\n  - `gold_fol_original`, `gold_fol_audited`;\n  - `gold_source` ∈ {original, panel_corrected, panel_flagged_uncorrected (the panel flagged the gold but no correction parsed/verified, so `gold_fol_audited`=null and the label is unknown unless L3-adjudicated), paper_corrected}. In the greedy rows: original 3,087, panel_corrected 2,160, paper_corrected 891, panel_flagged_uncorrected 162;\n  - `gold_fol_paper` + `paper_corrected_flag` (the 2606.02837 human correction);\n  - `gold_fol_refined` (yfxiao/folio-refined, metadata only).\n- **L0**: `gold_audit_votes` (per member: faithful, ambiguous, error types, explanation, model), `gold_faithful_final`, `sentence_ambiguous`, `gold_audit_primary_error`, `gold_audit_cascade_rule`, `gold_audit_full_panel_subset`, `gold_correction_status`.\n- **L1**, for both `L1_orig_*` and `L1_audited_*`:\n  - `status` ∈ {equiv_proved, equiv_bounded, non_equiv, non_equiv_no_bijection, unknown_capped, unknown_timeout, unparseable, no_audited_gold};\n  - `mapping` (gold→candidate bijection);\n  - `entail_cand_to_gold` / `entail_gold_to_cand` (bounded one-directional hints under the best mapping);\n  - `method` (bounded/unbounded), `max_domain`, `seconds`.\n- **L2**: `L2_status` ∈ {equiv_granular, non_equiv, not_applicable, not_run_equivalent}, `L2_definitions`, `L2_lexical=true`, `L2_orig_status`.\n- **L3**:\n  - `L3_selected`;\n  - `L3_votes` (per member: cand_faithful, gold_faithful, error_types, primary_error, ambiguous, same_meaning, model);\n  - `L3_majority`, `L3_primary_error`, `L3_dissent`, `L3_gold_shown_as`;\n  - `L3_sampling_weight` (post-stratified N_h/n_h), `L3_poststratum`, `L3_design_weight`, `L3_design_stratum`;\n  - `L3_stratum_p_faithful` (estimated panel-faithful share of this row's post-stratum; a label-noise prior for non-adjudicated rows).\n- **Complexity**: `n_tokens`, `n_quantifiers`, `nesting_depth`, `n_conditions`, `complexity_composite`, `complexity_tercile`, `complexity_gold_used`.\n- **`label_source`**; **`source_verified`** / **`source_file`** (set by `data.py`).\n\n## 5. How labels were built\n\n**Panel.** Every member is family-disjoint from all 9 generators (Meta, Qwen, Mistral, OpenAI, Google, DeepSeek, Microsoft) and from the gemini judge baseline.\n\n| Member | Model | Calibration gate (40 known-label items, ≥80% required) | Note |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| M1 | anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5 | 0.90 | The planned claude-sonnet-4.5 **failed** at 0.775: it rejected valid contrapositive and material-implication rewrites. The plan's fallback was used |\n| M2 | x-ai/grok-4.3 (reasoning low) | 0.90 | grok-4-fast is no longer in the OpenRouter catalog |\n| M3 | z-ai/glm-4.6, **non-thinking** | 0.875 | Chosen for cost; the low-reasoning config scored 0.925 |\n\nThe calibration set:\n- 15 ProverQA-easy golds;\n- 15 solver-verified non-equivalent mutants: negation flip, ∀↔∃, implication reversal, dropped conjunct, and ∧↔∨ (used because ProverQA-easy has no binary predicates for argument swaps);\n- 10 solver-verified equivalent rewrites: contrapositive, material implication, predicate renaming.\n\nThe gate uses the original, pre-registered labels, even though 3 of the \"faithful\" golds are defensibly wrong.\n\n**L0 / L4 gold audit.** A cascade: M2 and M3 vote on every gold; M1 votes on disagreements plus a seeded 20% subset. There were 264 M1 tie-breaks among 1,060 golds.\n\nA correction is accepted only if it:\n- parses;\n- is satisfiable (bounded);\n- has a bounded countermodel against the original.\n\nOtherwise it is rejected: 33 corrections were equivalent to the original, and some were unparseable because they used function terms. For the 99 MALLS items with a human correction, `gold_fol_audited` = the paper's gold.\n\n**L1** is lexical-free.\n- It checks the arity profile first. A mismatch gives `non_equiv_no_bijection`.\n- It then tries all bijections, capped at 5,040 (only 2 greedy items hit the cap).\n- Only countermodels count as proofs of non-equivalence.\n- `equiv_proved` needs an unbounded z3 `unsat`. All equivalences in this run were proved, so no `equiv_bounded` items occur.\n\nValidation on the 59 parseable pairs among the paper's first 60 MALLS items: every uncorrected pair was equivalent (37/37) and every corrected pair was non-equivalent (22/22). Mean cost is about 6 ms per pair.\n\n**L3** is blinded and symmetric: gold and candidate appear as Formula A / Formula B in seeded random order, with no source or system named.\n- The design sample was 460: 400 non-equivalent, with the top tercile oversampled 1.5×, including 40 of the 77 L2-granular items; plus 60 equivalent.\n- 150 extra items were drawn uniformly at random within the top-tercile non-equivalent strata.\n- Estimates use post-stratified weights on the final frame. The frame has 5,848 greedy parseable candidates with audited gold; every stratum has at least 3 adjudicated items.\n\n**E1.**\n- 203 sentences carried a named entity or constant: FOLIO 187, MALLS only 16, because MALLS gold is almost constant-free. So the planned 75/75 split became 187/16.\n- gpt-4.1-mini paraphrased each sentence and renamed its entities. The renaming was applied to the gold constants and predicate substrings.\n- M2 verified both the meaning and the renamed gold. 104 of 203 passed (target was ≥120). Most drops are \"the renamed gold is not faithful to the paraphrase\", which reflects residual gold problems.\n- 124 of 936 renamed candidates are flagged `contamination_rename_incomplete`.\n\n## 6. Deviations from the plan (honest list)\n\n1. **Corrected gold WAS found.** arXiv:2606.02837's release is on HF as `DSAVlab-UNIUD/MALLS_test_subset-CURATED` (100 items = MALLS-test indices 0–99, verified by NL match) and `DSAVlab-UNIUD/FOLIO_validation-curated` (275 FOLIO-v2-validation instances: 73 whole-story premise blocks plus 202 conclusions). The paper's GitHub repo is still 404.\n   - The FOLIO file corrects stories and v2 conclusions, not v1 premises. So the L4 panel-vs-human check covers only the 22 screen conclusions whose v1 gold equals the v2 gold the paper judged (κ = 0.18, n = 22: too small to conclude anything).\n2. **Panel substitutions**: haiku-4.5 for sonnet-4.5 (failed the gate), grok-4.3 for the unavailable grok-4-fast, glm-4.6 non-thinking. See §5.\n3. **ProverQA was audited in full** (the 10% escalation rule fired). Its flagged golds use panel-corrected gold (`gold_source=panel_corrected`).\n4. **L3 was enlarged** from 300 to 610 adjudicated items (460 design + 150 extra top-tercile), funded from the reserve. Weights are post-stratified.\n5. **A parser bug was found and fixed after L0.** v1 read constants such as `yr2028`/`p1080` as free variables and universally closed them. It affected 8 golds (4 held-out) and 9 candidates.\n   - The drawn sample was kept frozen.\n   - The 4 affected sentences' complexity features were recomputed with the stored z-parameters (the old values are in `complexity_v1_buggy`).\n   - All L1/L2 labels were recomputed from scratch; the old cache is kept as `work/l1_cache_v1_buggy.jsonl`.\n   - The panel corrections were re-derived (0 changed).\n   - E1 verification was re-keyed on content. This re-called all 203 verifications, costing $0.49 of waste.\n   - 3 L3 items changed stratum; post-stratification absorbs this.\n6. **Extractor v2**: phi-4 answered in LaTeX (`\\(\\forall x \\, \\text{P}(x)\\)`). A formatting fix, not a prompt change, raised its parse rate from 68% to 88%. All rows were re-extracted from the stored `raw_output`.\n7. **The ASCII `^` is read as ∧, not ⊕**, because LLM outputs use it as a look-alike of ∧.\n8. The **folio-refined** signal barely agrees with the panel: κ = 0.004 on 416 FOLIO items; refined differs from the original on 34%, the panel flags 51%. Its (undocumented) refinement targets something other than faithfulness, e.g. cross-story vocabulary alignment. It is kept as metadata only.\n9. **Class balance**: 62% of labelled greedy rows are unfaithful, above the 25–60% target. This is inflated by the L1 label noise described in §1. Per corpus: ProverQA 42%, MALLS 66%, FOLIO 75%.\n10. **\"Heavily conditioned\" means connective-heavy, not exception-heavy.** The public sentence-level gold corpora contain almost no `unless`/`except` sentences, so the top tercile is driven by multiple connectives, ⊕, ↔ and quantifier nesting. E2 (EU AI Act) is the only exception-rich material, and it is unlabeled.\n11. Temperature-0 output is not bit-deterministic across OpenRouter providers. The provider is recorded per row, and `raw_output` is the ground truth of what was scored.\n12. `kenken6696/folio_by_ccg2lambda` and `kenken6696/MALLS_by_ccg2lambda` were downloaded (to `temp/datasets/`) as possible iter-2 non-LLM systems but **not used**. Their neo-Davidsonian event FOL has no predicate bijection to gold, so they could only be adjudicated by the panel.\n\n## 7. Dataset search and selection (TODOs 2–5)\n\n**Search.** 40 broad HF queries (`temp/hf_search.txt`), including: folio, MALLS, ProverQA, first-order logic, FOL, autoformalization, logical reasoning, semantic parsing, ProofWriter, LogicNLI, theorem proving, lambda calculus, monotonicity, logic-lm, …\n\n**Previews.** 20 candidates were previewed through the HF datasets-server API (`temp/previews/candidate_previews.json`; the skill's own preview script lacked the `datasets` module). 14 provenance web searches are in `temp/websearch/`.\n\n**KEPT (10):**\n\n| Dataset | Downloads | Licence | Role |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| tasksource/folio | 6.2k | FOLIO MIT | FOLIO-v2 train/validation (yale-nlp/FOLIO is gated for this token) |\n| yuan-yang/MALLS-v0 | 158 | CC BY-NC 4.0 | MALLS-v0.1-test (Yang et al. 2023) |\n| DSAVlab-UNIUD/MALLS_test_subset-CURATED | 26 | — | Human corrections from arXiv:2606.02837. Low downloads, but provenance is verified: card, paper and authors |\n| DSAVlab-UNIUD/FOLIO_validation-curated | 61 | — | Same paper; FOLIO side |\n| opendatalab/ProverQA | 380 | — | ICLR 2025, dev easy/medium/hard via `hf_hub_download`; `load_dataset` breaks |\n| yfxiao/folio-refined | 20 | MIT | Independent wrong-gold signal (method undocumented; metadata only) |\n| Yale-LILY/FOLIO GitHub v0.0 | — | — | v1 validation/train (screen gold) |\n| teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM `outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_*.json` | — | — | Screen candidates |\n| User `dpv_pilot_study.zip` | — | — | E2 |\n| kenken6696/{folio,MALLS}_by_ccg2lambda | — | CC BY 4.0 | Downloaded for iter 2, unused |\n\n**DISCARDED**, each with its reason:\n\n| Dataset | Reason |\n|---|---|\n| benlipkin/folio, minimario/FOLIO, jhkim64/NL2FOL_sentence | Duplicate FOLIO mirrors, or <10 downloads with no card |\n| tasksource/FOL-nli (182 MB), tasksource/LogicNLI, tasksource/proofwriter, logicreasoning/logi_glue (963 MB), kkkarry/LogicGraph (698 MB) | NLI/QA with TPTP or proof annotations; no sentence-level NL→FOL gold, and some are too large |\n| Isotonic/symbolic_data_first_order_logic | Arithmetic \"FOL\"; no card |\n| verify-ppt/ppt-logic_first_order | No loadable data |\n| tasksource/monotonicity-entailment (MED) | Used by screen Arm A, not a FOL corpus |\n\n## 8. How to run (in order)\n\n```bash\nuv venv .venv --python=3.12 && uv pip install --python=.venv/bin/python z3-solver nltk aiohttp loguru huggingface_hub numpy scipy tenacity requests pyyaml pytest\nbash restore.sh                                    # sources + nltk data + pilot extraction\n.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q -c pytest.ini tests/test_fol.py\n.venv/bin/python src/prep_sources.py               # steps 1-3 (no API)\n.venv/bin/python src/generate.py --concurrency 24  # step 4 (~$0.72; resumable)\n.venv/bin/python src/reextract.py                  # extractor v2\n.venv/bin/python src/label_l1.py --stage orig\n.venv/bin/python src/calibrate.py && .venv/bin/python src/calibrate_variants.py   # (~$0.88)\n.venv/bin/python src/audit.py --cap 4.0            # L0 + L4 (~$3.43)\n.venv/bin/python src/fix_varlike_patch.py          # only needed for artefacts produced by parser v1\n.venv/bin/python src/label_l1.py --stage audited\n.venv/bin/python src/l3_adjudicate.py --n-non 400 --n-eq 60 --cap 2.8   # (~$2.46)\n.venv/bin/python src/l3_extra.py --n 150 --cap 0.85                      # (~$0.88)\n.venv/bin/python src/e1_contamination.py --n 203 --cap 0.75              # (~$0.86)\n.venv/bin/python src/e2_transfer.py\n.venv/bin/python src/assemble.py                  # -> work/assembled_data_out.json.gz + label_report.json\nuv run data.py                                     # source verification -> full_data_out.json (validated: exp_sel_data_out)\n.venv/bin/python src/split_outputs.py             # -> full_data_out/full_data_out_{1,2,3}.json + mini/preview\n```\n\nEvery panel and generation response is cached (`work/panel_cache.jsonl`, `raw/generations/`), so a re-run of the labelling steps costs $0.\n\nTotal spend: **$9.22**:\n\n| Phase | Cost |\n|---|---|\n| Generation | $0.72 |\n| Calibration | $0.88 |\n| Gold audit | $3.43 |\n| L3 | $2.46 + $0.88 |\n| E1 | $0.86 |\n\nIt stayed under the $9.30 hard cap, with the ledger updated after every call.\n\n## 9. Restoring removed files\n\nThese paths are deleted after the round (see `.aii/manifest.yaml`). `bash restore.sh` recreates all of them. It also re-downloads `raw/hf_downloads/` and the ccg2lambda files in `temp/datasets/` if they are missing; those are small and are kept.\n\n| Path | Restore with |\n|---|---|\n| `raw/pilot/` | `python -c \"import zipfile; zipfile.ZipFile('/ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/user_uploads/dpv_pilot_study.zip').extractall('raw/pilot')\"` |\n| `nltk_data/` | `python -c \"import nltk; [nltk.download(p, download_dir='nltk_data') for p in ('wordnet','omw-1.4')]\"` |\n| `.venv/` | The `uv venv …` line in §8 |\n| `__pycache__/`, `.pytest_cache/` | Regenerated automatically |\n", "numLines": 291, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 291}}

{"type": "text", "file": {"filePath": "/ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_1/src/fol_parse.py", "content": "\"\"\"FOLIO-syntax FOL parser -> hashable tuple AST, printer, and AST utilities.\n\nGrammar (Unicode, FOLIO / Logic-LM style):\n  quantifiers ∀x ∃x (variables: any identifier bound by a quantifier)\n  connectives ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕ with precedence ¬ > ∧ > ∨ > → > ↔,⊕ ; → is right-associative\n  atoms Name(t1, ..., tk), zero-arity atoms Name, equalities t1 = t2, t1 ≠ t2\n  terms: identifiers only (constants may contain digits/underscores/unicode letters); no function terms.\nAnything else (∈, ⊆, ≤, '::', ForAll(, LaTeX, nested function terms) raises ParseError.\n\nQuantifier scope: if the quantifier is followed by '(' the scope is that parenthesised group\n(FOLIO convention); otherwise (∀x Dog(x) → Bark(x)) the scope extends as far right as possible.\nUnbound single-letter lowercase arguments (x, y, z, x1 ...) are treated as FREE VARIABLES and\nuniversally closed (flag free_vars=True). All other unbound arguments are constants.\n\nAST node forms (tuples, hashable):\n  ('atom', name, (term, ...))  term = ('v', name) | ('c', name)\n  ('eq', t1, t2)\n  ('not', f)\n  ('and', (f1, f2, ...)) / ('or', (f1, f2, ...))   n-ary, flattened\n  ('imp', a, b) / ('iff', a, b) / ('xor', a, b)\n  ('all', var, f) / ('ex', var, f)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nTOKEN_RE = re.compile(r\"\\s*(∀|∃|¬|∧|∨|→|↔|⊕|≠|=|\\(|\\)|,|\\w+|\\S)\", re.U)\nBINOPS = {\"↔\": (1, \"iff\"), \"⊕\": (1, \"xor\"), \"→\": (2, \"imp\"), \"∨\": (3, \"or\"), \"∧\": (4, \"and\")}\nFREE_VAR_RE = re.compile(r\"^[a-z]\\d*$\")", "numLines": 30, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 349}}

Score = 1 - weighted fraction of mismatching coordinates:\n        concept polarity mismatches, dropped text concepts, added/vacuous formula predicates, swapped role\n        anchors (relation slot i anchored to the wrong argument), relativized coordination coordinates.\n      Returns a score in [0,1] (0.5 if uncovered), a predicted error type, and the mismatch list.\n      variant=\"A1\": the text polarity comes from substitution-entailment questions to an LLM\n      (google/gemini-2.5-flash, thinking budget 1024; needs OPENROUTER_API_KEY; ~$0.0025 per sentence).\n  alignment_only(text, fol)   A0 ablation: dropped/added coordinates only (no polarity, no anchors)\n  concept_coverage(text, fol) Ccov control: fraction of text content concepts covered by formula symbols\n\nWHAT THE SCORE MEASURES: agreement between (a) which content words of the sentence occur in upward vs\ndownward-entailing positions (restrictor/antecedent/negated vs scope/consequent), which argument slot each\nrole word fills, and which words are represented at all, and (b) the same properties computed exactly\nfrom the formula. It is invariant (by construction) under logical equivalence, conjunct reordering,\ncontrapositive, De Morgan, prenexing; it is NOT invariant under predicate renaming that breaks the lexical\nalignment, and it is blind (pre-registered) to quantifier-scope swaps, cardinality and (for A1) and/or swaps.\n\nPIPELINE (python method.py --stages all): reproduces every artifact of the screen:\n  build_screen_set -> run_sigs -> run_text(pilot, probe, b6) -> run_baselines(b1, b1_mutants, b3, b4)\n  -> run_selfcons -> run_metrics -> analyze   (LLM calls are cached in results/llm_cache; $ cap 5.80)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport asyncio\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom loguru import logger\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / \"src\"))\n\nlogger.remove()\nlogger.add(sys.stdout, level=\"INFO\", format=\"{time:HH:mm:ss}|{level:<7}|{message}\")\n(ROOT / \"logs\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)\nlogger.add(ROOT / \"logs\" / \"method.log\", rotation=\"30 MB\", level=\"DEBUG\")\n\n\ndef _text_side(text: str, variant: str) -> tuple[dict, dict]:\n    import text_sig\n    ex = text_sig.extract(text)\n    if variant == \"A3\" or variant in (\"A0\", \"Ccov\"):\n        a3_rel = {}\n        for c, q, kind in ex[\"coord\"]:\n            for (x, y) in ((c, q), (q, c)):\n                lx = ex[\"marker\"].get(x, \"+\")\n                if kind == \"and\":\n                    a3_rel[(x, y, \"out\")], a3_rel[(x, y, \"in\")] = \"0\", lx\n                elif kind == \"or\":\n                    a3_rel[(x, y, \"in\")], a3_rel[(x, y, \"out\")] = \"0\", lx\n        return ex, {\"labels\": res = asyncio.run(llm.run_batch([dict(model=\"google/gemini-2.5-flash\",\n                                          messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt}], purpose=\"api_probe\",\n text_sig.parse_answers(res[0]))\n    return ex, {\"labels\": T[\"concept_labels\"], \"anchors\": ex[\"anchors\"],\n                \"rel\": T[\"rel_labels\"] if variant == \"A2\" else {}}\n\n\ndef signature_faithfulness(text: str, fol: str, variant: str = \"A3\") -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Gold-free faithfulness score of `fol` for `text`. variant in {A1, A2, A3, A0, Ccov}.\"\"\"", "numLines": 75, out += [{\"item_id\": r[\"rid\"], \"set\": \"rewrite\", \"sid\": r[\"sid\"], \"nl\": S[r[\"sid\"]][\"nl\"], \"fol\": r[\"fol\"],\n                 \"kind\": r[\"kind\"]} for r in d[\"rewrites\"]]\n = \"MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli\" if dev == \"cuda\" else \\\n        \"MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-base-mnli-fever-anli\"\n    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(name)\n    mdl = AutoModelForSequenceClassification.from_pretrained(name).to(dev).eval()\n    ent = [k for k, v in mdl.config.id2label.items() if v.lower().startswith(\"entail\")][0]\n\n    def p_ent(prem, hyp):\n        out = []\n        for i in range(0, len(prem), 32):\n            b = tok(prem[i:i + 32], hyp[i:i + 32], return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True, truncation=True, max_length=256).to(dev)\n   "implementation_pseudocode": "=== S0. SETUP (about 20 min) ===\nWS = /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/3_invention_loop/iter_2/gen_art/<this workspace>   # all writes here\n\nEnvironment:\nuv venv .venv --python 3.12\nuv pip install z3-solver numpy pandas scipy scikit-learn loguru aiohttp tenacity nltk spacy \\\n    sentence-transformers transformers torch crowd-kit pyyaml\nThen install whatever Arm A's text_sig needs. Read Arm A's pyproject/README for the spaCy model name, e.g. `uv run python -m spacy download en_core_web_sm`, and the nltk wordnet data.\n\nVENDOR (copy, never edit):\n- vendor/armA/ <- Arm A method.py + src/*.py\n- vendor/armB/ <- Arm B src/*.py\n- vendor/ds/   <- dataset src/fol_parse.py, fol_granular.py, fol_equiv.py, or_client.py\nWrite results/frozen_manifest.json = {path: sha1} for every vendored file, plus sha1(B1_PROMPT), sha1(B3_PROMPT) and the A1 probe template. Re-verify at the end; the run fails if anything changed.\n\n!! MODULE-NAME COLLISIONS: Arm A and Arm B both have src/llm.py, and Arm A and the dataset both have src/fol_parse.py.\n- Run each arm's scorer in its OWN subprocess with its own sys.path: workers/score_armA.py, workers/score_armB_pairs.py, workers/adapter.py.\n- Exchange data by JSONL files in work/.\n- Never import both arms into one interpreter.\n\nCOST CONTROL:\n- src/or_client.py (from the dataset's client): async aiohttp; semaphore 16; retries with tenacity.\n- The ledger work/cost_ledger.jsonl is appended after EVERY call: phase, model, tokens, usd, seconds.\n- global HARD_CAP = 9.50 USD, checked before every call; per-phase caps as in S4.\n- Before each sweep: est = n_calls * pilot_usd_per_call; log it; abort the phase if spent + est > phase cap.\n- All LLM calls use this client with the EXACT iter-1 request params. Iter-1 llm.py clients are not used for spend, but their prompt builders/parsers are.\n\n=== S1. LOAD & FRAME ===\nrows = concat of the examples in full_data_out_{1,2,3}.json (README loader, parts sorted numerically)\nG  = heldout_confirm rows (expect 6,300; 700 sentence_ids x 9 systems; assert)\nS  = heldout_samples (7,000), keyed (sentence_id, system, sample_idx)\nC  = contamination (936); metadata_original_item_id -> G row\nSA = screen_gold_audit (1,003)\nT  = transfer_unlabeled (367)\n\nFor each G row keep: item_id = metadata_item_id; sid = metadata_sentence_id; system; sentence and candidate_fol from json.loads(input); parse_ok; corpus; complexity_tercile; n_conditions; n_tokens; n_quantifiers; nesting_depth; story_id; L1_audited_status; L2_status; label_source; output; L3_majority; L3_primary_error; L3_dissent; L3_votes; L3_sampling_weight; L3_design_weight; L3_design_stratum; L3_poststratum; L3_stratum_p_faithful.\n\nLabels:\n- y_panel = 1 if (label_source=='panel3' and output=='faithful'), 0 if (label_source=='panel3' and output=='unfaithful'), else NA. Expect 609; count and log any panel 'unknown'.\n- y_solver = output mapped over ALL labelled rows. This field is the audited-solver/L1 label except on panel rows, so ALSO build y_solver_pure from L1_audited_status: equiv_* -> 1; non_equiv*, with gold_source=='original' ProverQA using L1_orig per README precedence -> 0; unknown otherwise. Use y_solver_pure on the 609 panel items for circularity test (a).\n- q_soft (tertiary):\n  - panel items: y_panel;\n  - L1-equivalent items: 1 - 0.099 (label_report value; read it from label_report.json, not hard-coded);\n  - L1-non-equivalent items: L3_stratum_p_faithful;\n  - unparseable: 0;\n  - unknown: NA.\n- w = L3_sampling_weight (post-stratified N_h/n_h) for panel analyses. Sensitivity uses L3_design_weight. Report Kish n_eff = (sum w)^2 / sum w^2.\n\n=== S2. INPUT ADAPTER (the iter-1 parsers accept only the FOLIO unicode dialect) ===\nIn workers/adapter.py (dataset parser only), for each candidate string in G, S, C, T and SA:\n    pr = ds.fol_parse.parse(s)\n    canon = ds.fol_parse.to_str(pr.ast) if ok else None\nWrite work/canon.jsonl {key, raw, canon, ds_parse_ok}.\nThen, in each arm's subprocess, test armB fc.try_parse(canon or raw) and armA parse(canon or raw).\n\nCoverage table: ds_parse_ok, armA_ok(raw), armA_ok(canon), armB_ok(raw), armB_ok(canon) per corpus x system.\n- Metrics are scored on canon when available, else raw.\n- An item unparseable by the metric's own parser gets score 0.5 and covered=False (LC gives 0.0 per its frozen code for its own unparseable; KEEP that and report it).\n- Log adapter-induced changes in results/deviations.json.\n\n=== S3. ZERO-LLM METRICS (CPU; ProcessPoolExecutor, spawn, max_workers = n_cpu - 1) ===\n3a. LC PAIRS (Arm B subprocess workers):\n    For each sid, for all 36 pairs of the 9 systems whose candidates parse:\n        find_bijections(a, b, wall_s=15)   # frozen defaults: cap 5040, max_preds 8\n        pairs[sid]['a|b'] = {'bij': label}\n    Cache in work/pairs_greedy.jsonl, keyed by sha1(canonA || canonB), so identical strings are never recomputed and the job is resumable.\n    Log label counts: equiv / nonequiv / unlabeled (too_many_preds, too_many_maps, timeout, solver_unknown).\n    Budget about 25k pair calls. Measure throughput on the mini run first. If the projected time is > 90 min, run only the sentences needed first: the panel sentences plus all top-tercile ones, then fill the rest. The EM needs all sentences for the final fit, so the final fit runs after all pairs finish.\n\n3b. LC FIT: build ss = {'sentences': [{sid, tercile}], 'real_items': [{item_id: f'{sid}:{system}', sid, system, parse_ok: armB_ok}]}, then call latent_class.run_latent_class(ss, pairs, alts=[]) VERBATIM.\n    This emits LC_onecoin, LC_onecoin_str (the trigram key: include the 'str' labels only if the build_screen trigram map is reproduced; otherwise skip and log), LC_maj, LC_huiwalter (tercile groups) and LC_ds_binary.\n    Do NOT use the gold-as-rater variant for AUROC.\n    Save the fit info: p_w per system, pi, rho, degenerate flag.\n    Map item ids back to metadata_item_id.\n\n3c. LC_WITHIN, for gpt-4.1-mini and llama-3.1-8b separately:\n    raters = {greedy, s0..s4} of ONE system\n    compute the 15 pairs per sid with the same find_bijections; cache in work/pairs_within.jsonl\n    call em() through the same build_obs path, with 'systems' = the 6 rater slots\n    score = posterior of the greedy item's class\n    Compare LC_within with LC_onecoin on the SAME 2x700 greedy items (panel subset and all).\n\n3d. B8 self-consistency = (# of the 5 samples bij-equivalent to greedy) / (# parseable samples). Reuse the 3c pairs. Report n and coverage. Uncovered (greedy unparseable or 0 parseable samples) -> 0.5.\n\n3e. LC_granular (POST-HOC LABEL):\n    for each pair labelled nonequiv with reason vocab_mismatch or no_equiv_map:\n        ds.fol_granular.granular_equivalence(astA, astB, time_limit=10)   # both directions\n        merge the classes if either direction says equiv\n    Rerun em(). This is gold-free (candidate vs candidate). Cap the total at 40 CPU-min, and report how many pairs it touched.\n\n3f. SIGNATURE (Arm A subprocess): for every G row, every C row and every T row:\n        signature_faithfulness(sentence, canon_or_raw, variant='A3')\n        signature_faithfulness(..., 'A0')\n        signature_faithfulness(..., 'Ccov')\n    Keep score, covered, predicted error type and mismatch list. The formula signature is cached per canonical string, and the text side per sentence.\n    Expect about 2.6k signatures in 19 s per iter-1, so this is minutes.\n\n3g. B2 = parse_ok, using the dataset's parse_ok as B2 AND armB_ok as B2_armB.\n\n3h. B7 structural (ported; no declared predicate lists in held-out). Per item:\n    - B7_arity_self = no predicate used with two arities in the formula;\n    - B7_arity_story = no predicate conflicts in arity with the same system's other formulas in the same story_id. FOLIO-train/ProverQA have story_id; MALLS items are singletons, so this is always 1 there. Use the per-source grouping if story_id is null;\n    - B7_joint = z3 satisfiability of the conjunction of all of the system's formulas in that story (bounded grounding via vendor/ds or armB fc, 5 s timeout; unknown -> 1);\n    - B7_jacc = mean predicate-name Jaccard between greedy and the 5 samples (2 systems only);\n    - B7 = int(parse_ok and B7_arity_self and B7_arity_story and B7_joint), mirroring iter-1's composite.\n    Report each component as its own metric too.\n\n=== S4. LLM METRICS (in priority order; spend estimated before each sweep) ===\n4a. B1 cheap judge on all 6,300 G rows plus all 936 C rows.\n    request = EXACT b1_request_example.json: google/gemini-2.5-flash, T=0.0, max_tokens 16, reasoning {max_tokens:0, exclude:true}\n    prompt = B1_PROMPT.format(s=sentence, f=candidate_as_generated)\n    Use the RAW extracted candidate string, as iter-1 fed the Logic-LM string. Also record a flag if raw != canon.\n    Retry unparseable numbers with max_tokens 48, as iter-1 did.\n    CACHE-CONSISTENCY: key = sha1(prompt). Identical prompts (duplicate candidates across systems) are called ONCE and the score is reused. Report the duplicate share.\n    TEST-RETEST: re-call 300 random unique prompts sequentially (concurrency 1). Report Spearman, mean |diff| and the share of |diff| > 0.2. The primary score stays the first pass.\n    Estimated about 6.5k unique calls x about $0.0001 ≈ $0.7-1.2. Phase cap $1.8.\n\n4b. A1 gemini probes:\n    for each unique sentence in G (700) and paraphrase in C (104):\n        qs = text_sig.probe_questions(text_sig.extract(text))\n        prompt = text_sig.render_prompt(qs)\n        call gemini-2.5-flash, reasoning {max_tokens: 1024}, max_tokens 4000 (Arm A's exact params)\n        T = text_sig.labels_from_answers(ex, qs, text_sig.parse_answers(resp))\n    Save the text-side JSON per sentence. Then, in the Arm A subprocess, compute signature_score(T, S, A, variant='A1') for every candidate of that sentence, reusing formula sigs and alignments.\n    Implement this as workers/a1_apply.py, which mirrors method.signature_faithfulness except that _text_side takes the cached T. This re-wiring is logged; the code path is otherwise identical.\n    Estimated about 804 calls x $0.0025 ≈ $2.0. Phase cap $2.4. Pilot 20 calls first; if $/call > 0.004, restrict A1 to panel sentences plus top-tercile sentences.\n\n4c. B3 round-trip + Monty-style conformance. Items: the 609 panel items + the contamination slice.\n    Contamination slice = C rows of systems {gpt-4.1-mini, deepseek-v3.1, llama-3.1-8b} and their originals: about 312 + 312 minus overlap.\n    back = gemini-2.5-flash B3_PROMPT, reasoning 0, max_tokens 200; first line kept\n    B3cos = MiniLM cosine(sentence, back)\n    B3nli = min(P_ent(s -> back), P_ent(back -> s)) with DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli on CUDA\n    B3c (Monty-style, NEW, labelled re-implementation): gemini-2.5-flash, T=0, reasoning 0, with the prompt:\n        'List the atomic claims (clauses) of TEXT A, then of TEXT B. For each clause of A say whether B expresses it; for each clause of B say whether A expresses it. Return JSON {a_clauses:[...], a_covered:[bool], b_clauses:[...], b_covered:[bool]}'\n        A = sentence, B = back\n        score = harmonic mean of the two coverage shares\n    Estimated about 1.2k back-translations x $0.0002 + 1.2k conformance calls x $0.0004 ≈ $0.75. Phase cap $1.0.\n\n4d. B1+ frontier judge with the SAME B1_PROMPT. Items: the 609 panel items + the same contamination slice (about 1.2k calls).\n    Model order:\n    - (1) google/gemini-2.5-pro, reasoning {max_tokens: 128}, max_tokens 256;\n    - (2) openai/gpt-5, reasoning effort low;\n    - (3) anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5 is NOT allowed (panel family); x-ai and z-ai are NOT allowed.\n    Pilot 20 calls, then n_affordable = floor(3.0 / usd_per_call). If fewer than 1.2k calls fit, keep all 609 panel items and subsample contamination pairs by sentence.\n    Record the family overlap: gemini-2.5-pro shares the Google family with generator gemini-2.5-flash, gpt-5 shares the OpenAI family with gpt-4.1-mini and gpt-oss. Report B1+ AUROC separately on own-family vs other-family items (self-preference check).\n    Phase cap $3.0.\n\n4e. Gold-recall probe (cheap, about $0.05): for the 104 original contamination sentences, ask gemini-2.5-flash (T=0, reasoning 0):\n    'This sentence is from a public NL-to-FOL benchmark. Write its reference first-order-logic annotation exactly as it appears in the dataset.'\n    Measure the predicate-name token overlap and the exact-string match with gold_fol_original. Compare with the same prompt on the 104 paraphrases (renamed entities) as the baseline. A higher overlap on originals means recall.\n\n4f. RESERVE ≈ $1.0-1.5, never spent automatically. Optional if spend < $7: extend B3/B3c to all 6,300 for system-level tau.\n\n=== S5. ANALYSES (analysis.py; all CIs = 1000x bootstrap resampling sentence_ids WITHIN L3_design_stratum for panel analyses and within corpus x tercile for all-item analyses; seed 0) ===\nMETRICS = [LC_onecoin, LC_huiwalter, LC_maj, LC_ds_binary, LC_within(2 sys), LC_granular*, A3, A1, A0, Ccov, B1, B1plus, B2, B3cos, B3nli, B3c, B7 (+ components), B8(2 sys)]\n\n5.1 Item-level AUROC:\n    - (P) weighted AUROC on panel items: sklearn roc_auc_score(y_panel, score, sample_weight=w), cross-checked by a hand pairwise implementation;\n    - (S) unweighted AUROC vs y_solver on all labelled G rows;\n    - (T) expected AUROC with soft labels, where each item enters as positive with weight q and as negative with weight 1-q (pairwise formula);\n    - (U) panel unanimous subset (L3_dissent == False);\n    - (D) design-weight sensitivity.\n    Within-sentence AUROC = the mean over sentences having both classes (P if n_sent >= 30, else S labels, with n reported).\n    Coverage = covered share over all 6,300 (unparseable counted); mean $ and seconds per item (LC seconds = its pair time amortised per candidate + the EM share).\n\n5.2 Pre-registered C1-C3 (verbatim), per metric, on panel items:\n    - C1: weighted AUROC >= 0.70;\n    - C2: delta-AUROC of the cross-fitted logistic [B1 + parse_ok + best other baselines + metric] over the same model without the metric, 95% lower bound > 0;\n    - C3: coverage >= 70% with unscored = 0.5.\n    'Best other baselines' is FROZEN NOW as [B1, B2, B3nli, B3cos, B7]. B1plus is excluded from the base because it is a cost-comparison target; a second table adds B1plus to the base.\n    CONFIRMED iff C1 & C2 & C3. Also report each under solver labels (secondary). If LC confirms only under solver labels, write 'agrees with gold vocabulary', not 'faithfulness'.\n\n5.3 Stacking (panel items, weights w):\n    - 5 sentence-grouped folds x 5 shuffles; standardised features; sklearn LogisticRegression(C=1.0, lbfgs, max_iter 2000); OOF probabilities averaged over shuffles;\n    - delta-AUROC = AUROC(OOF_with) - AUROC(OOF_without), CI from resampling sentences on the OOF vectors;\n    - models: base; base+LC_onecoin; base+A3; base+A1; base+A0; base+A3 over base+A0 (polarity increment); base+LC+A3; base+LC+A3+A1; base+all;\n    - B8 table: the same on the gpt-4.1-mini + llama panel subset, with B8 in the base; report n;\n    - placebo: a random-uniform feature must give CI ∋ 0.\n\n5.4 LC CIRCULARITY TESTS:\n    (a) on the SAME 609 items: AUROC(LC, y_panel, w) vs AUROC(LC, y_solver_pure, w); the paired bootstrap difference and CI;\n    (b) AUROC(LC, y_panel, w) restricted to panel items with L1_audited_status in {non_equiv, non_equiv_no_bijection} (expect about 546; about 44% faithful), with CI; the same for A3, A1, B1, B1plus, B3 as comparators;\n    (c) among panel-faithful & solver-non-equivalent items: the share with LC_onecoin < 0.5, and the share in a minority class (class size < largest class) or a singleton; compare with the same shares among panel-unfaithful non-equivalent items;\n    (d) the same (b) restricted to non_equiv_no_bijection only (the strict-arity mismatch cases).\n    Pre-registered reading:\n    - 'not a checker artefact' iff the (b) AUROC 95% lower bound > 0.5 AND the point estimate >= 0.60;\n    - 'partial' iff lower bound > 0.5 but point < 0.60;\n    - 'artefact' iff the CI includes 0.5.\n\n5.5 System level:\n    truth_s = mean over the system's 700 items of q_i, where unparseable = 0 and unknown = the stratum/corpus mean q (report the unknown count)\n    metric_s = mean score (uncovered = 0.5)\n    Report Kendall tau-b(metric_s, truth_s), pairwise accuracy over the 36 system pairs, and a sentence-bootstrap CI (recomputing both truth and metric in each replicate).\n    Panel-only metrics (B1plus, B3*) use panel items with weights w.\n    The report says tau is underpowered with n=9 and is not a gate.\n\n5.6 Complexity:\n    - AUROC per complexity_tercile and per n_conditions bin {0-1, 2-3, 4+}, under P and S, with n per cell;\n    - pre-registered crossover vs B1 for each of LC_onecoin, A3, A1, B1plus, B3nli: gap_t = AUROC_m - AUROC_B1 per tercile; the slope = OLS of gap on tercile index 1..3, computed inside each bootstrap replicate -> CI;\n    - CROSSOVER CONFIRMED iff slope CI > 0 AND top-tercile gap lower bound > 0;\n    - also the logistic interaction y ~ z(s_m) + z(s_B1) + tercile + z(s_m):tercile + z(s_B1):tercile; test b[s_m:t] - b[s_B1:t] with a clustered bootstrap;\n    - primary population = panel (weighted); powered secondary = all labelled items under y_solver and under q_soft.\n\n5.7 Contamination (per LLM metric B1, B1plus, B3cos, B3nli, B3c, A1, plus A3 as a zero-LLM control):\n    delta_i = s(original item) - s(paraphrase row), paired over C rows\n    Report the mean delta with a 104-sentence cluster CI, and AUROC on originals vs on paraphrases (label transferred) with a paired bootstrap.\n    Exclude contamination_rename_incomplete rows as a sensitivity check.\n    Plus the gold-recall probe overlap (4e).\n    Prediction recorded in advance: A1 and A3 delta ≈ 0; B1 may drop.\n\n5.8 Judge reliability: the B1 test-retest stats; B1 duplicate-prompt share; B1plus own-family vs other-family AUROC.\n\n=== S6. SCREEN RE-RANK (pre-registered iter-1 rule, verbatim from Arm A analyze.SELECTION_RULE) ===\n- Load both arms' results/screen_scores.jsonl and screen_set.json.\n- Build the join key = (norm(sentence), system_norm), where norm = the lowercase, whitespace-collapsed, punctuation-stripped sentence, and system_norm maps {gpt-3.5-turbo, gpt-4, text-davinci-003} across the arms' naming.\n- Join to SA rows (metadata_sentence_id / metadata_item_id; sentence from input). Keep metadata_in_screen_first300 == True. EXCLUDE stories 87/105/106/173 (Arm A premise-zip misalignment; match via Arm B screen_set story ids).\n- Report the join rate per arm; expect about 833-835 matched items minus the exclusions; log the unmatched ones.\n- y_aud = SA output (faithful=1, unfaithful=0, unknown excluded and counted).\n- For each candidate metric in the rule: Arm A {A1, A2, A3} (A0 and Ccov are controls) and Arm B {TVJT, NLI_deberta, LC_onecoin}. Recompute under y_aud:\n    - G1 coverage (label-free, recomputed on the joined frame);\n    - G2 = COPY the iter-1 values: rewrites are label-independent, so no recomputation. State the iter-1 absolute definition and also the paired FA_paired_absdiff_gt_0.2 where available;\n    - G3 = OOF delta over [B1, parse_ok] with the iter-1 oof_delta code, using each arm's own B1 column;\n    - G4 = AUROC >= 0.65.\n- Rank survivors by top-tercile AUROC (SA complexity_tercile); apply the 0.03 winner margin, the top-two Spearman < 0.5 stack rule, and the no-survivor clause exactly.\n- Do this (i) per arm and (ii) jointly on the items both arms share (the governing ranking).\n- Output results/screen_rerank.json {per_metric gates under iter-1 labels vs audited labels, ranking_before, ranking_after, changed: bool, governing_outcome}.\n- Flag: y_aud is still solver-equivalence against audited gold, so it does not test LC circularity.\n\n=== S7. TRANSFER (qualitative) ===\n- A3 on the 367 T rows. Report coverage (289 parse expected; the 66 ⊆ rows are unparseable and counted) and the distribution of A3-predicted error types.\n- Compare against A3-predicted types on held-out panel-unfaithful items and against the panel L3_primary_error mix.\n- Split by grounded vs ungrounded pilot runs if that metadata exists; purely descriptive, no accuracy claim.\n- LC is not applicable (single system), so say so.\n\n=== S8. OUTPUTS ===\n- results/heldout_scores.jsonl: one line per (item_id, metric): {item_id=metadata_item_id, fold, metric, score, covered, usd, seconds, extra}. Include C and T rows; the join key is shared with sibling artifacts.\n- results/analysis.json: every table above with CIs and n.\n- results/confirmation_table.json: metric x {AUROC_P, CI, AUROC_S, AUROC_T, AUROC_U, coverage, C1, C2 delta+CI, C3, CONFIRMED, top-tercile AUROC, crossover verdict, contamination delta, $/item, s/item}.\n- results/circularity.json, results/system_level.json, results/screen_rerank.json, results/transfer_A3.json, results/deviations.json, results/frozen_manifest.json, work/cost_ledger.jsonl.\n- method.py: the single entry point `uv run method.py --stage {mini,zero_llm,llm,analysis,rerank,transfer,all}`. Resumable, because every stage reads its caches.\n- method_out.json in the exp_gen_sol_out schema: per-example predictions for the G rows with metric scores as predict_* fields, plus the metadata summary. Validate it with the aii-json skill, then make the mini/preview variants and split it if it is > the size limit (aii-file-size-limit skill).\n- README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml: keep results/, work/ caches (irreproducible API outputs) and vendor/; delete .venv/, the HF model cache and __pycache__ as redownloadable/regenerable, with source commands.",
  "fallback_plan": "(1) Iter-1 file unreadable or import breaks: first try running it as a subprocess with the arm's original ROOT layout reproduced under vendor/. If that fails, re-implement from the direction text and the docstrings read here (one-coin EM likelihood in latent_class.py; the A3 score = 1 - weighted mismatch fraction), mark the metric 're-implemented' in deviations.json, and demote it from CONFIRM claims to 'indicative'.\n\n(2) The reproduction pre-flight fails. The vendored LC does not reproduce Arm B's screen LC_onecoin within 1e-6 from data/candidate_pairs.json, or vendored A3 does not reproduce Arm A's screen A3 on 50 items. Diagnose (environment, nltk/spaCy model version, random seeds) for at most 30 min. If a small drift remains (|diff| < 0.01 on 95% of items), proceed and report the drift. Otherwise report both and use the vendored output.\n\n(3) LC pair checks are too slow (projected > 90 min at 6 workers): pairs are cached and resumable.\n- Priority order: the panel sentences, then top-tercile sentences, then the rest.\n- If still short, fit EM on the sentences completed and score only those, reporting the fraction. Never change wall_s/max_preds (frozen).\n- LC_granular is the first thing dropped, then LC_within for llama; then B8 uses only the 5 greedy-vs-sample pairs (cheap).\n\n(4) Adapter yields low coverage for an arm (< 70%): report the metric under C3 failing. Do not add parser rules to iter-1 code. Show the coverage failure broken down by syntax cause (LaTeX, ASCII, function terms, ⊆). This is a legitimate negative about portability.\n\n(5) Budget pressure. The order of sacrifice:\n- B3c Monty-style;\n- the gold-recall probe;\n- B1+ contamination slice (keep B1+ on the 609 panel items);\n- A1 restricted to panel + top-tercile sentences;\n- B3 restricted to panel items only;\n- B1 on contamination restricted to the 3-system slice.\nB1 on all 6,300 and the zero-LLM metrics are never sacrificed. The hard stop is $9.50.\n\n(6) Frontier model unavailable or too expensive (> $0.005/call): the fallback chain is gemini-2.5-pro -> openai/gpt-5 (low) -> openai/gpt-5-mini (medium), marked 'not frontier'. If none fits, report B1+ skipped, with a reason.\n\n(7) OpenRouter daily limit or outage (seen in other runs): all calls are cached. Stop LLM stages, finish the zero-LLM analyses, write partial LLM results with coverage flags, and resume when the key recovers. The analysis code must tolerate missing metrics.\n\n(8) Panel labels too few in a tercile/bin (< 40 items or < 10 per class): report 'insufficient n' for that cell and rely on the S/T labellings for the powered version, stated as secondary.\n\n(9) EM degenerate on held-out (p_w > 0.99 or pi < 0.02; the degenerate flag in the info): report it, use the best non-degenerate restart if one exists, and else fall back to LC_maj as the LC representative (pre-stated here).",
  "testing_plan": "T0 UNIT (no API, under 10 min):\n(a) Adapter round-trip on 30 held-out candidates of each corpus: to_str(parse(x)) re-parses to the same AST in vendor/ds, and armA/armB accept the canon form. Print the 10 worst failures.\n(b) Weighted AUROC: the hand pairwise implementation equals sklearn roc_auc_score(sample_weight) to 1e-9 on random data. With soft labels q in {0,1} it equals the hard AUROC.\n(c) Bootstrap: resampling sentence ids within strata keeps each stratum's sentence count.\n(d) A toy LC with 3 sentences x 3 systems via latent_class.em gives posteriors in [0,1] with classes consistent with the pairs.\n(e) B1 prompt: the sha1 of the formatted template equals the Arm B prompts_frozen sha1 of B1_PROMPT; a request dict diffed against b1_request_example.json shows only the content differing.\n\nT1 REPRODUCTION PRE-FLIGHT, which proves the code is frozen. Then, in the Arm A subprocess, compute signature_score(T, S, A, variant='A1') for every candidate of that sentence, reusing formula sigs and alignments.\\n    Implement this as workers/a1_apply.py, which mirrors method.signature_faithfulness except that _text_side takes the cached T. This re-wiring is logged; the code path is otherwise identical.\\n    Estimated about 804 calls x $0.0025 ≈ $2.0. Phase cap $2.4. Pilot 20 calls first; if $/call > 0.004, restrict A1 to panel sentences plus top-tercile sentences.\\n\\n4c. B3 round-trip + Monty-style conformance. Items: the 609 panel items + the contamination slice.\\n    Contamination slice = C rows of systems {gpt-4.1-mini, deepseek-v3.1, llama-3.1-8b} and their originals: about 312 + 312 minus overlap.\\n    back = gemini-2.5-flash B3_PROMPT, reasoning 0, max_tokens 200; first line kept\\n    B3cos = MiniLM cosine(sentence, back)\\n    B3nli = min(P_ent(s -> back), P_ent(back -> s)) with DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli on CUDA\\n    B3c (Monty-style, NEW, labelled re-implementation): gemini-2.5-flash, T=0, reasoning 0, with the prompt:\\n        'List the atomic claims (clauses) of TEXT A, then of TEXT B. For each clause of A say whether B expresses it; for each clause of B say whether A expresses it. Return JSON {a_clauses:[...], a_covered:[bool], b_clauses:[...], b_covered:[bool]}'\\n        A = sentence, B = back\\n        score = harmonic mean of the two coverage shares\\n    Estimated about 1.2k back-translations x $0.0002 + 1.2k conformance calls x $0.0004 ≈ $0.75. Phase cap $1.0.\\n\\n4d. B1+ frontier judge with the SAME B1_PROMPT. Items: the 609 panel items + the same contamination slice (about 1.2k calls).\\n    Model order:\\n    - (1) google/gemini-2.5-pro, reasoning {max_tokens: 128}, max_tokens 256;\\n    - (2) openai/gpt-5, reasoning effort low;\\n    - (3) anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5 is NOT allowed (panel family); x-ai and z-ai are NOT allowed.\\n    Pilot 20 calls, then n_affordable = floor(3.0 / usd_per_call). If fewer than 1.2k calls fit, keep all 609 panel items and subsample contamination pairs by sentence.\\n    Record the family overlap: gemini-2.5-pro shares the Google family with generator gemini-2.5-flash, gpt-5 shares the OpenAI family with gpt-4.1-mini and gpt-oss. Report B1+ AUROC separately on own-family vs other-family items (self-preference check).\\n    Phase cap $3.0.\\n\\n4e. Gold-recall probe (cheap, about $0.05): for the 104 original contamination sentences, ask gemini-2.5-flash (T=0, reasoning 0):\\n    'This sentence is from a public NL-to-FOL benchmark. Write its reference first-order-logic annotation exactly as it appears in the dataset.'\\n    Measure the predicate-name token overlap and the exact-string match with gold_fol_original. Compare with the same prompt on the 104 paraphrases (renamed entities) as the baseline. A higher overlap on originals means recall.\\n\\n4f. RESERVE ≈ $1.0-1.5, never spent automatically. Optional if spend < $7: extend B3/B3c to all 6,300 for system-level tau.\\n\\n=== S5. ANALYSES (analysis.py; all CIs = 1000x bootstrap resampling sentence_ids WITHIN L3_design_stratum for panel analyses and within corpus x tercile for all-item analyses; seed 0) ===\\nMETRICS = [LC_onecoin, LC_huiwalter, LC_maj, LC_ds_binary, LC_within(2 sys), LC_granular*, A3, A1, A0, Ccov, B1, B1plus, B2, B3cos, B3nli, B3c, B7 (+ components), B8(2 sys)]\\n\\n5.1 Item-level AUROC:\\n    - (P) weighted AUROC on panel items: sklearn roc_auc_score(y_panel, score, sample_weight=w), cross-checked by a hand pairwise implementation;\\n    - (S) unweighted AUROC vs y_solver on all labelled G rows;\\n    - (T) expected AUROC with soft labels, where each item enters as positive with weight q and as negative with weight 1-q (pairwise formula);\\n    - (U) panel unanimous subset (L3_dissent == False);\\n    - (D) design-weight sensitivity.\\n    Within-sentence AUROC = the mean over sentences having both classes (P if n_sent >= 30, else S labels, with n reported).\\n    Coverage = covered share over all 6,300 (unparseable counted); mean $ and seconds per item (LC seconds = its pair time amortised per candidate + the EM share).\\n\\n5.2 Pre-registered C1-C3 (verbatim), per metric, on panel items:\\n    - C1: weighted AUROC >= 0.70;\\n    - C2: delta-AUROC of the cross-fitted logistic [B1 + parse_ok + best other baselines + metric] over the same model without the metric, 95% lower bound > 0;\\n    - C3: coverage >= 70% with unscored = 0.5.\\n    'Best other baselines' is FROZEN NOW as [B1, B2, B3nli, B3cos, B7]. B1plus is excluded from the base because it is a cost-comparison target; a second table adds B1plus to the base.\\n    CONFIRMED iff C1 & C2 & C3. Also report each under solver labels (secondary). If LC confirms only under solver labels, write 'agrees with gold vocabulary', not 'faithfulness'.\\n\\n5.3 Stacking (panel items, weights w):\\n    - 5 sentence-grouped folds x 5 shuffles; standardised features; sklearn LogisticRegression(C=1.0, lbfgs, max_iter 2000); OOF probabilities averaged over shuffles;\\n    - delta-AUROC = AUROC(OOF_with) - AUROC(OOF_without), CI from resampling sentences on the OOF vectors;\\n    - models: base; base+LC_onecoin; base+A3; base+A1; base+A0; base+A3 over base+A0 (polarity increment); base+LC+A3; base+LC+A3+A1; base+all;\\n    - B8 table: the same on the gpt-4.1-mini + llama panel subset, with B8 in the base; report n;\\n    - placebo: a random-uniform feature must give CI ∋ 0.\\n\\n5.4 LC CIRCULARITY TESTS:\\n    (a) on the SAME 609 items: AUROC(LC, y_panel, w) vs AUROC(LC, y_solver_pure, w); the paired bootstrap difference and CI;\\n    (b) AUROC(LC, y_panel, w) restricted to panel items with L1_audited_status in {non_equiv, non_equiv_no_bijection} (expect about 546; about 44% faithful), with CI; the same for A3, A1, B1, B1plus, B3 as comparators;\\n    (c) among panel-faithful & solver-non-equivalent items: the share with LC_onecoin < 0.5, and the share in a minority class (class size < largest class) or a singleton; compare with the same shares among panel-unfaithful non-equivalent items;\\n    (d) the same (b) restricted to non_equiv_no_bijection only (the strict-arity mismatch cases).\\n    Pre-registered reading:\\n    - 'not a checker artefact' iff the (b) AUROC 95% lower bound > 0.5 AND the point estimate >= 0.60;\\n    - 'partial' iff lower bound > 0.5 but point < 0.60;\\n    - 'artefact' iff the CI includes 0.5.\\n\\n5.5 System level:\\n    truth_s = mean over the system's 700 items of q_i, where unparseable = 0 and unknown = the stratum/corpus mean q (report the unknown count)\\n    metric_s = mean score (uncovered = 0.5)\\n    Report Kendall tau-b(metric_s, truth_s), pairwise accuracy over the 36 system pairs, and a sentence-bootstrap CI (recomputing both truth and metric in each replicate).\\n    Panel-only metrics (B1plus, B3*) use panel items with weights w.\\n    The report says tau is underpowered with n=9 and is not a gate.\\n\\n5.6 Complexity:\\n    - AUROC per complexity_tercile and per n_conditions bin {0-1, 2-3, 4+}, under P and S, with n per cell;\\n    - pre-registered crossover vs B1 for each of LC_onecoin, A3, A1, B1plus, B3nli: gap_t = AUROC_m - AUROC_B1 per tercile; the slope = OLS of gap on tercile index 1..3, computed inside each bootstrap replicate -> CI;\\n    - CROSSOVER CONFIRMED iff slope CI > 0 AND top-tercile gap lower bound > 0;\\n    - also the logistic interaction y ~ z(s_m) + z(s_B1) + tercile + z(s_m):tercile + z(s_B1):tercile; test b[s_m:t] - b[s_B1:t] with a clustered bootstrap;\\n    - primary population = panel (weighted); powered secondary = all labelled items under y_solver and under q_soft.\\n\\n5.7 Contamination (per LLM metric B1, B1plus, B3cos, B3nli, B3c, A1, plus A3 as a zero-LLM control):\\n    delta_i = s(original item) - s(paraphrase row), paired over C rows\\n    Report the mean delta with a 104-sentence cluster CI, and AUROC on originals vs on paraphrases (label transferred) with a paired bootstrap.\\n    Exclude contamination_rename_incomplete rows as a sensitivity check.\\n    Plus the gold-recall probe overlap (4e).\\n    Prediction recorded in advance: A1 and A3 delta ≈ 0; B1 may drop.\\n\\n5.8 Judge reliability: the B1 test-retest stats; B1 duplicate-prompt share; B1plus own-family vs other-family AUROC.\\n\\n=== S6. SCREEN RE-RANK (pre-registered iter-1 rule, verbatim from Arm A analyze.SELECTION_RULE) ===\\n- Load both arms' results/screen_scores.jsonl and screen_set.json.\\n- Build the join key = (norm(sentence), system_norm), where norm = the lowercase, whitespace-collapsed, punctuation-stripped sentence, and system_norm maps {gpt-3.5-turbo, gpt-4, text-davinci-003} across the arms' naming.\\n- Join to SA rows (metadata_sentence_id / metadata_item_id; sentence from input). Keep metadata_in_screen_first300 == True. EXCLUDE stories 87/105/106/173 (Arm A premise-zip misalignment; match via Arm B screen_set story ids).\\n- Report the join rate per arm; expect about 833-835 matched items minus the exclusions; log the unmatched ones.\\n- y_aud = SA output (faithful=1, unfaithful=0, unknown excluded and counted).\\n- For each candidate metric in the rule: Arm A {A1, A2, A3} (A0 and Ccov are controls) and Arm B {TVJT, NLI_deberta, LC_onecoin}. Recompute under y_aud:\\n    - G1 coverage (label-free, recomputed on the joined frame);\\n    - G2 = COPY the iter-1 values: rewrites are label-independent, so no recomputation. State the iter-1 absolute definition and also the paired FA_paired_absdiff_gt_0.2 where available;\\n    - G3 = OOF delta over [B1, parse_ok] with the iter-1 oof_delta code, using each arm's own B1 column;\\n    - G4 = AUROC >= 0.65.\\n- Rank survivors by top-tercile AUROC (SA complexity_tercile); apply the 0.03 winner margin, the top-two Spearman < 0.5 stack rule, and the no-survivor clause exactly.\\n- Do this (i) per arm and (ii) jointly on the items both arms share (the governing ranking).\\n- Output results/screen_rerank.json {per_metric gates under iter-1 labels vs audited labels, ranking_before, ranking_after, changed: bool, governing_outcome}.\\n- Flag: y_aud is still solver-equivalence against audited gold, so it does not test LC circularity.\\n\\n=== S7. TRANSFER (qualitative) ===\\n- A3 on the 367 T rows. Report coverage (289 parse expected; the 66 ⊆ rows are unparseable and counted) and the distribution of A3-predicted error types.\\n- Compare against A3-predicted types on held-out panel-unfaithful items and against the panel L3_primary_error mix.\\n- Split by grounded vs ungrounded pilot runs if that metadata exists; purely descriptive, no accuracy claim.\\n- LC is not applicable (single system), so say so.\\n\\n=== S8. OUTPUTS ===\\n- results/heldout_scores.jsonl: one line per (item_id, metric): {item_id=metadata_item_id, fold, metric, score, covered, usd, seconds, extra}. Include C and T rows; the join key is shared with sibling artifacts.\\n- results/analysis.json: every table above with CIs and n.\\n- results/confirmation_table.json: metric x {AUROC_P, CI, AUROC_S, AUROC_T, AUROC_U, coverage, C1, C2 delta+CI, C3, CONFIRMED, top-tercile AUROC, crossover verdict, contamination delta, $/item, s/item}.\\n- results/circularity.json, results/system_level.json, results/screen_rerank.json, results/transfer_A3.json, results/deviations.json, results/frozen_manifest.json, work/cost_ledger.jsonl.\\n- method.py: the single entry point `uv run method.py --stage {mini,zero_llm,llm,analysis,rerank,transfer,all}`. If that fails, re-implement from the direction text and the docstrings read here (one-coin EM likelihood in latent_class.py; the A3 score = 1 - weighted mismatch fraction), mark the metric 're-implemented' in deviations.json, and demote it from CONFIRM claims to 'indicative'.\\n\\n(2) The reproduction pre-flight fails. The vendored LC does not reproduce Arm B's screen LC_onecoin within 1e-6 from data/candidate_pairs.json, or vendored A3 does not reproduce Arm A's screen A3 on 50 items. Diagnose (environment, nltk/spaCy model version, random seeds) for at most 30 min. If a small drift remains (|diff| < 0.01 on 95% of items), proceed and report the drift. Otherwise report both and use the vendored output.\\n\\n(3) LC pair checks are too slow (projected > 90 min at 6 workers): pairs are cached and resumable.\\n- Priority order: the panel sentences, then top-tercile sentences, then the rest.\\n- If still short, fit EM on the sentences completed and score only those, reporting the fraction. Never change wall_s/max_preds (frozen).\\n- LC_granular is the first thing dropped, then LC_within for llama; then B8 uses only the 5 greedy-vs-sample pairs (cheap).\\n\\n(4) Adapter yields low coverage for an arm (< 70%): report the metric under C3 failing. This is a legitimate negative about portability.\\n\\n(5) Budget pressure. The order of sacrifice:\\n- B3c Monty-style;\\n- the gold-recall probe;\\n- B1+ contamination slice (keep B1+ on the 609 panel items);\\n- A1 restricted to panel + top-tercile sentences;\\n- B3 restricted to panel items only;\\n- B1 on contamination restricted to the 3-system slice.\\nB1 on all 6,300 and the zero-LLM metrics are never sacrificed. The hard stop is $9.50.\\n\\n(6) Frontier model unavailable or too expensive (> $0.005/call): the fallback chain is gemini-2.5-pro -> openai/gpt-5 (low) -> openai/gpt-5-mini (medium), marked 'not frontier'. With soft labels q in {0,1} it equals the hard AUROC.\\n(c) Bootstrap: resampling sentence ids within strata keeps each stratum's sentence count.\\n(d) A toy LC with 3 sentences x 3 systems via latent_class.em gives posteriors in [0,1] with classes consistent with the pairs.\\n(e) B1 prompt: the sha1 of the formatted template equals the Arm B prompts_frozen sha1 of B1_PROMPT; a request dict diffed against b1_request_example.json shows only the content differing.\\n\\nT1 REPRODUCTION PRE-FLIGHT, which proves the code is frozen. This module wrote the plan for the executor. It ran no code.

### [98] ASSISTANT · 2026-09-23 13:57:43 UTC

```
I've written the plan for the frozen held-out confirmation experiment to `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`. Nothing was run, since this step only plans. I checked the file's escaping and quoting by pattern search; it was not parsed as JSON.

**What the plan asks the executor to do:**
- **Re-run everything frozen.** The executor copies the iter-1 Arm A and Arm B code and the dataset parser into its workspace, records a sha1 for every file, and checks them again at the end. Before the held-out run, it must reproduce the iter-1 screen scores for latent class (LC) and the zero-LLM signature (A3) from the copied code.
- **Metrics:** LC_onecoin, LC_huiwalter, LC_maj, LC_within and LC_granular (flagged as added after the fact). The signature variants A3, A1 and A0. The baselines B1 (cheap judge on all 6,300 items plus the 936 contamination rows), B1+ (frontier judge), B2 parse, B3 round-trip, B7 structural and B8 self-consistency.
- **Labels:** the primary target is the 609 panel-adjudicated items with their post-stratified weights. Every headline is also reported under the audited-solver labels and the soft stratum labels.
- **Pre-registered criteria:** C1–C3 applied verbatim, plus the crossover test against the B1 judge across complexity terciles.
- **Latent-class circularity tests (a)–(d):** LC scored against panel vs solver labels on the same items; LC's AUROC inside the roughly 546 solver-non-equivalent panel items; where panel-faithful but gold-inequivalent candidates sit among LC's classes; and a strict-arity subset. The verdict thresholds are written down in advance.
- **Other analyses:** stacking increments, system-level Kendall tau with pairwise accuracy (9 systems is too few for this to be a pass/fail criterion), a paired contamination check with a gold-recall probe, the iter-1 G1–G4 screen re-ranking under audited labels, and the A3 error-type distribution on the EU-AI-Act pilot.
- **Budget:** about $7 planned, with a hard stop at $9.50 in code and a written order of what to drop first under budget pressure.

**Problems in the iter-1 code that the plan works around:**
- **Parser dialect.** Both iter-1 parsers only accept FOLIO-style unicode formulas, so they would reject many MALLS and ProverQA candidates. The plan passes candidates through the dataset's more tolerant parser first and reports coverage with and without that step.
- **Name clashes.** Arm A and Arm B both have `src/llm.py`, and Arm A and the dataset both have `src/fol_parse.py`. Loading them in one Python process would silently pick the wrong module, so each arm runs in its own subprocess.

**Changes I made to the direction:**
- **Compute profile:** `gpu_basic` rather than a CPU tier. Iter-1's round-trip baseline used the large DeBERTa model on GPU, and the extra cores speed up the roughly 46k latent-class equivalence checks.
- **Added a baseline:** a Monty-style clause-conformance check. The field expects it, and it costs about $0.3 because it reuses the round-trip back-translations.
- **A1 costs more than the direction assumed:** about $2, not $0.5, based on iter-1's cost per sentence.

The `practice_alignment` field lists where the plan departs from field practice and what each departure costs. Examples: panel labels rather than human ones, B7 re-defined because the held-out outputs have no declared predicate lists, and a system-level tau with too little power to be a gate.

I also added a README, an empty manifest and a memory note to the workspace.
```
